# Changed test expectations — R22 Phase A

Baseline: `8c456709`. This inventory covers every changed pre-existing test file from `git diff 8c456709 -- '*test*' '*tests*'`, plus changed JSON fixtures directly consumed by those tests. New test files are not pre-existing expectations.

Counts: **14 test files**, **68 assertion or test-input changes**, **1 consumed JSON fixture**, **615 semantic fixture changes**; **682 JUSTIFIED**, **1 UNJUSTIFIED / RESTORED**, **0 FLAGGED**.

Each row identifies a changed assertion or an unchanged assertion whose fixture/input or resolver changed. Added assertions in pre-existing files are included. Imports, comments and test-title edits alone are not assertion changes. Old/main line numbers are supplied where the expression itself changed; fixture-driven rows point to the current consuming assertion.

R22 restored execution isolation in Core enrichment and catalog normalization. The two PR-added Core modality tests were corrected to assert specifications.modalityFacts instead of requiring top-level execution modalities to equal display facts. Those tests did not exist at 8c456709 and therefore do not inflate the pre-existing-file count. No pre-existing display assertion became unnecessary merely by restoring execution isolation.

R6A had an unjustified blanket exception derived from archived full-object output. It is removed: ordinary retention assertions are restored, and only twelve explicitly pinned schema omissions across nine identities remain allowed. Their existing source records and dates are checked directly. No unresolved FLAGGED expectations remain.

The frozen execution fixture is generated by scripts/freeze-execution-capability.mjs from git show 8c456709 through an in-memory loader. It covers every static identity and every full-registry identity in the offline scenario, all execution fields outside the four display projections, catalog normalization, Core reference modes, Web vision/refine decisions, and real Mini settings/index decisions. Four new per-end tests compare current outputs to this fixture.

The picker-name fixture and tests are preserved: all 1064 static names must equal main. Existing historical fixture JSON files under tests/fixtures are unchanged. Newly added R21/phase-2 evidence and report JSON, the name fixture, the R22 execution fixture, and the twelve-case schema fixture are new files rather than edits to pre-existing fixtures.

All evidence references are local archived records or code rules. This review made no network requests and does not newly verify channel availability, account entitlement, model quality or HTTP status.

## Verification

`node scripts/report-changed-tests.mjs --check` independently recomputes the changed pre-existing file list, checks directly consumed JSON fixtures, checks every diff hash and review count, and verifies this Markdown matches the JSON. A negative test removes a reviewed file and requires the guard to fail.
